        function showRegion(regionId, lang) {
            const data = regionData[regionId];
            if (!data) return;
 
            document.getElementById(`region-default-${lang}`).classList.add('hidden');
            const contentBox = document.getElementById(`region-content-${lang}`);
            contentBox.classList.remove('hidden');
 
            if (data.sites) {
                let html = '';
                data.sites.forEach((site, index) => {
                    const name = lang === 'zh' ? site.name_zh : site.name_en;
                    const address = lang === 'zh' ? site.address_zh : site.address_en;
                    const business = lang === 'zh' ? site.business_zh : site.business_en;
                    
                    const labelName = lang === 'zh' ? '🏢 名　　稱：' : '🏢 Name：';
                    const labelAddress = lang === 'zh' ? '📍 詳細地址：' : '📍 Address：';
                    const labelBusiness = lang === 'zh' ? '⚙️ 核心業務：' : '⚙️ Business：';
 
                    html += `
                        <div class="site-block ${index > 0 ? 'mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-indigo-500/20' : ''}">
                            <h4 class="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-indigo-400 mb-3 border-b border-indigo-500/30 pb-2 flex items-center">
                                <span class="mr-2">${labelName}</span>
                                <span class="text-white light-mode-title">${name}</span>
                            </h4>
                            <div class="space-y-3 text-sm md:text-base">
                                <p class="flex items-start">
                                    <span class="font-bold text-gray-300 light-mode-title min-w-[100px] shrink-0">${labelAddress}</span> 
                                    <span class="text-gray-400 light-mode-desc font-medium leading-relaxed">${address}</span>
                                </p>
                                <p class="flex items-start">
                                    <span class="font-bold text-gray-300 light-mode-title min-w-[100px] shrink-0">${labelBusiness}</span> 
                                    <span class="text-gray-400 light-mode-desc font-medium leading-relaxed">${business}</span>
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
                contentBox.innerHTML = html;
            } else {
                const labelName = lang === 'zh' ? '🏢 名　　稱：' : '🏢 Name：';
                const labelAddress = lang === 'zh' ? '📍 詳細地址：' : '📍 Address：';
                const labelBusiness = lang === 'zh' ? '⚙️ 核心業務：' : '⚙️ Business：';
 
                const name = lang === 'zh' ? data.name_zh : data.name_en;
                const address = lang === 'zh' ? data.address_zh : data.address_en;
                const business = lang === 'zh' ? data.business_zh : data.business_en;
 
                contentBox.innerHTML = `
                    <h4 class="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-indigo-400 mb-3 border-b border-indigo-500/30 pb-2 flex items-center">
                        <span class="mr-2">${labelName}</span>
                        <span id="region-name-${lang}" class="text-white light-mode-title">${name}</span>
                    </h4>
                    <div class="space-y-3 text-sm md:text-base">
                        <p class="flex items-start">
                            <span class="font-bold text-gray-300 light-mode-title min-w-[100px] shrink-0">${labelAddress}</span> 
                            <span class="text-gray-400 light-mode-desc font-medium leading-relaxed" id="region-address-${lang}">${address}</span>
                        </p>
                        <p class="flex items-start">
                            <span class="font-bold text-gray-300 light-mode-title min-w-[100px] shrink-0">${labelBusiness}</span> 
                            <span class="text-gray-400 light-mode-desc font-medium leading-relaxed" id="region-business-${lang}">${business}</span>
                        </p>

                `;
            }
 
            contentBox.classList.remove('animate-fade-in');
            void contentBox.offsetWidth;
            contentBox.classList.add('animate-fade-in');

        let activeIndex = 1; 

        // 品牌動態介面名稱對照表
        const brandPageTitles = {
            zh: [
                "組員介紹",
                "公司介紹",
                "市場分析",
                "資源能力分析",
                "商業模式",
                "人力職缺"
            ],
            en: [
                "Team Members",
                "Company Introduction",
                "Market Analysis",
                "Resources & Capabilities",
                "Business Model",
                "Jobs"
            ]
        };

        function updateBrandTitle() {
            const companyNameEl = document.querySelectorAll(".brandCompanyName");
            const currentPageEl = document.querySelectorAll(".brandCurrentPage");
            const separatorEl = document.querySelectorAll(".brand-page-separator");

            const isEn = currentLang === "en";
            const contentContainer = document.getElementById('content-container');
            const isContentPage = contentContainer && !contentContainer.classList.contains('hidden');

            companyNameEl.forEach(el => {
                el.textContent = isEn ? "UMC" : "聯華電子";
            });

            currentPageEl.forEach(el => {
                if (isContentPage) {
                    el.textContent = brandPageTitles[isEn ? "en" : "zh"][activeIndex] || "";
                    el.classList.remove("hidden");
                } else {
                    el.textContent = "";
                    el.classList.add("hidden");
                }
            });

            separatorEl.forEach(el => {
                if (isContentPage) {
                    el.classList.remove("hidden");
                } else {
                    el.classList.add("hidden");
                }
            });
        }

        function initCarousel() {
            const track = document.getElementById('carousel-track');
            track.innerHTML = '';
            SECTIONS.forEach((item, index) => {
                const titleText = currentLang === 'zh' ? item.title_zh : item.title_en;
                const subTitleText = currentLang === 'zh' ? item.subtitle_zh : item.subtitle_en;
                
                const subTitleHtml = currentLang === 'zh' 
                    ? `<span class="text-[10px] md:text-xs tracking-wider uppercase text-blue-500 font-semibold mb-1 md:mb-2 block">${subTitleText}</span>` 
                    : '';
                
                const titleFontSizeClass = currentLang === 'zh' 
                    ? 'text-xl sm:text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black' 
                    : 'text-base sm:text-lg md:text-xl font-bold tracking-tight px-2 leading-tight';

                const cardHtml = `
                    <div id="carousel-item-${index}" 
                         onclick="handleCardClick(${index})"
                         class="carousel-item absolute w-[65vw] sm:w-[280px] md:w-[320px] p-4 md:p-6 rounded-2xl glass-panel text-center cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-center items-center h-[25vh] max-h-[220px] min-h-[160px]">
                        <div class="flex justify-center mb-2 md:mb-4"><div class="p-3 md:p-4 icon-wrapper rounded-full">${item.icon}</div></div>
                        <div class="w-full">
                            ${subTitleHtml}
                            <h3 class="${titleFontSizeClass} text-white light-mode-title transition-all duration-300">${titleText}</h3>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                track.innerHTML += cardHtml;
            });
            updateCarousel();
            setupTouchGestures(); 
        }

        function handleCardClick(index) {
            if (carouselDragJustHappened) {
                return;
            }
            if (index === activeIndex) {
                enterCurrentSection(); 
            } else {
                setActiveIndex(index); 
            }
        }

        function updateCarousel() {
            SECTIONS.forEach((item, index) => {
                const card = document.getElementById(`carousel-item-${index}`);
                if (!card) return;
                let diff = index - activeIndex;
                const total = SECTIONS.length;
                if (diff < -total / 2) diff += total;
                else if (diff > total / 2) diff -= total;

                let transformStyle = '', opacity = 0, zIndex = 0, pointerEvents = 'none';

                if (diff === 0) {
                    transformStyle = 'translateX(0) scale(1.15) translateZ(80px) rotateY(0deg)';
                    opacity = 1; zIndex = 20; pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    card.style.borderColor = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.5)';
                    card.style.boxShadow = '0 15px 35px -10px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.4)';
                } else if (diff === -1) {
                    transformStyle = 'translateX(-110%) scale(0.8) translateZ(-50px) rotateY(35deg)';
                    opacity = 0.5; zIndex = 10; pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    card.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)'; card.style.boxShadow = 'none';
                } else if (diff === 1) {
                    transformStyle = 'translateX(110%) scale(0.8) translateZ(-50px) rotateY(-35deg)';
                    opacity = 0.5; zIndex = 10; pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    card.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)'; card.style.boxShadow = 'none';
                } else {
                    transformStyle = diff < 0 
                        ? 'translateX(-220%) scale(0.6) translateZ(-150px) rotateY(55deg)' 
                        : 'translateX(220%) scale(0.6) translateZ(-150px) rotateY(-55deg)';
                    opacity = 0; zIndex = 0; pointerEvents = 'none';
                    card.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)'; card.style.boxShadow = 'none';
                }
                card.style.transform = transformStyle; card.style.opacity = opacity;
                card.style.zIndex = zIndex; card.style.pointerEvents = pointerEvents;
            });
            updateBrandTitle();
        }

        function setActiveIndex(index) {
            if (index < 0) activeIndex = SECTIONS.length - 1;
            else if (index >= SECTIONS.length) activeIndex = 0;
            else activeIndex = index;
            updateCarousel();
        }

        function prevSlide() { setActiveIndex(activeIndex - 1); }
        function nextSlide() { setActiveIndex(activeIndex + 1); }

        let carouselTouchWheelLocked = false;
        let lastTouchWheelChangeTime = 0;
        const TOUCH_WHEEL_LOCK_MS = 650;
        let carouselDragJustHappened = false;

        function changeCarouselFromTouchWheel(direction, source = 'unknown') {
            const now = Date.now();

            if (
                carouselTouchWheelLocked ||
                now - lastTouchWheelChangeTime < TOUCH_WHEEL_LOCK_MS
            ) {
                return;
            }

            carouselTouchWheelLocked = true;
            lastTouchWheelChangeTime = now;

            if (direction === 'next') {
                nextSlide();
            } else {
                prevSlide();
            }

            setTimeout(() => {
                carouselTouchWheelLocked = false;
            }, TOUCH_WHEEL_LOCK_MS);
        }

        let isTouchDevice = false;
        window.addEventListener('touchstart', () => { isTouchDevice = true; }, { passive: true });

        let holdInterval = null;
        let holdTimeout = null;

        function startHold(direction, event) {
            if (event) {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
            }

            changeCarouselFromTouchWheel(direction, 'hold-start');

            clearTimeout(holdTimeout);
            clearInterval(holdInterval);

            holdTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
                holdInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    changeCarouselFromTouchWheel(direction, 'hold-repeat');
                }, TOUCH_WHEEL_LOCK_MS);
            }, 550);
        }

        function stopHold() {
            clearTimeout(holdTimeout);
            clearInterval(holdInterval);
            holdTimeout = null;
            holdInterval = null;
        }

        function setupTouchGestures() {
            const viewport = document.getElementById('carousel-viewport');
            if (!viewport || viewport.dataset.gestureBound === 'true') return;

            viewport.dataset.gestureBound = 'true';

            let startX = 0;
            let startY = 0;
            let currentX = 0;
            let currentY = 0;
            let pointerDown = false;
            let hasTriggered = false;
            let activePointerId = null;

            const swipeThreshold = 70;
            const verticalTolerance = 45;

            viewport.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
                if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') return;

                pointerDown = true;
                hasTriggered = false;
                activePointerId = event.pointerId;

                startX = event.clientX;
                startY = event.clientY;
                currentX = event.clientX;
                currentY = event.clientY;

                try {
                    viewport.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
                } catch (error) {}
            });

            viewport.addEventListener('pointermove', (event) => {
                if (!pointerDown || event.pointerId !== activePointerId) return;

                currentX = event.clientX;
                currentY = event.clientY;

                const diffX = currentX - startX;
                const diffY = currentY - startY;

                if (Math.abs(diffX) > Math.abs(diffY) && Math.abs(diffX) > 12) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                }

                if (hasTriggered) return;

                const isHorizontalSwipe =
                    Math.abs(diffX) >= swipeThreshold &&
                    Math.abs(diffX) > Math.abs(diffY) + verticalTolerance;

                if (isHorizontalSwipe) {
                    hasTriggered = true;
                    carouselDragJustHappened = true;

                    if (diffX > 0) {
                        changeCarouselFromTouchWheel('prev', 'pointer-swipe');
                    } else {
                        changeCarouselFromTouchWheel('next', 'pointer-swipe');
                    }

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        carouselDragJustHappened = false;
                    }, 350);
                }
            }, { passive: false });

            function endPointer(event) {
                if (!pointerDown || event.pointerId !== activePointerId) return;

                pointerDown = false;

                try {
                    viewport.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
                } catch (error) {}

                activePointerId = null;

                setTimeout(() => {
                    hasTriggered = false;
                }, TOUCH_WHEEL_LOCK_MS);
            }

            viewport.addEventListener('pointerup', endPointer);
            viewport.addEventListener('pointercancel', endPointer);
        }

        function handleHover(isEnter, id) {
            if (isTouchDevice) return; 
            const submenu = document.getElementById(`submenu-${id}`);
            const chevron = document.getElementById(`chevron-${id}`);
            if (isEnter) {
                submenu.style.maxHeight = '500px';
                chevron.classList.add('rotate-180');
            } else {
                submenu.style.maxHeight = '0';
                chevron.classList.remove('rotate-180');
            }
        }

        function toggleMenu(id) {
            const submenu = document.getElementById(`submenu-${id}`);
            const chevron = document.getElementById(`chevron-${id}`);
            if (submenu.style.maxHeight === '500px') {
                submenu.style.maxHeight = '0';
                chevron.classList.remove('rotate-180');
            } else {
                submenu.style.maxHeight = '500px';
                chevron.classList.add('rotate-180');
            }
        }

        function toggleSidebar() {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const overlay = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay');
            if (!sidebar.classList.contains('-translate-x-full')) {
                sidebar.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
                overlay.classList.add('opacity-0');
                setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.add('hidden'), 300);
            } else {
                overlay.classList.remove('hidden');
                setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.remove('opacity-0'), 10);
                sidebar.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');
            }
        }

        function closeSidebar() {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const overlay = document.getElementById('sidebar-overlay');
            sidebar.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
            overlay.classList.add('opacity-0');
            setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.add('hidden'), 300);
            
            ['com', 'market', 'resource', 'growth'].forEach(id => {
                const submenu = document.getElementById(`submenu-${id}`);
                if (submenu) submenu.style.maxHeight = '0';
                document.getElementById(`chevron-${id}`)?.classList.remove('rotate-180');
            });
        }

        function jumpToSection(index, elementId = null) {
            activeIndex = index;
            updateCarousel();
            enterCurrentSection();
            closeSidebar();
            if (elementId) {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const targetEl = document.getElementById(elementId);
                    const scrollArea = document.getElementById('content-scroll-area');
                    if (targetEl && scrollArea) {
                        const y = targetEl.getBoundingClientRect().top - scrollArea.getBoundingClientRect().top + scrollArea.scrollTop - 24;
                        scrollArea.scrollTo({top: y, behavior: 'smooth'});
                    }
                }, 400); 
            }
        }

        let wheelAccumulator = 0;
        let wheelResetTimeout = null;
        const WHEEL_THRESHOLD = 120;
        const WHEEL_RESET_MS = 180;

        window.addEventListener('wheel', function(event) {
            const contentContainer = document.getElementById('content-container');

            if (contentContainer && !contentContainer.classList.contains('hidden')) return;
            if (carouselTouchWheelLocked) return;

            const absX = Math.abs(event.deltaX);
            const absY = Math.abs(event.deltaY);
            const dominantDelta = absX > absY ? event.deltaX : event.deltaY;

            if (Math.abs(dominantDelta) < 8) return;

            wheelAccumulator += dominantDelta;

            clearTimeout(wheelResetTimeout);
            wheelResetTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
                wheelAccumulator = 0;
            }, WHEEL_RESET_MS);

            if (Math.abs(wheelAccumulator) >= WHEEL_THRESHOLD) {
                if (wheelAccumulator > 0) {
                    changeCarouselFromTouchWheel('next', 'wheel');
                } else {
                    changeCarouselFromTouchWheel('prev', 'wheel');
                }

                wheelAccumulator = 0;
                clearTimeout(wheelResetTimeout);
            }
        }, { passive: true });

        window.addEventListener('keydown', function(event) {
            const isContentOpen = !document.getElementById('content-container').classList.contains('hidden');
            if (isContentOpen) {
                if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") switchContentSection(-1);
                else if (event.key === "ArrowRight") switchContentSection(1);
                else if (event.key === "Escape") goToHome();
            } else {
                if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") prevSlide();
                else if (event.key === "ArrowRight") nextSlide();
                else if (event.key === "Enter") enterCurrentSection();
            }
        });

        document.body.addEventListener('click', () => window.focus());

        function toggleFlipCard(card) {
            card.classList.toggle('flipped');
            card.setAttribute('aria-pressed', card.classList.contains('flipped') ? 'true' : 'false');
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
            if (!(event.target instanceof Element)) return;
            const card = event.target.closest('.flip-card');
            if (!card) return;

            toggleFlipCard(card);
        });

        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(event) {
            if (!(event.target instanceof Element)) return;
            const card = event.target.closest('.flip-card');
            if (!card || (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ')) return;

            event.preventDefault();
            toggleFlipCard(card);
        });

        function initJobsSection() {
            const section = document.querySelector('.jobs-section');
            if (!section) return;

            renderJobCards();

            if (window.lucide) {
                lucide.createIcons();
            }
        }

        function renderJobCards() {
            for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
                const container = document.getElementById(`job-category-${i}`);
                if (container) container.innerHTML = '';
            }

            jobOpenings.forEach(job => {
                const container = document.getElementById(`job-category-${job.category}`);
                if (container) container.appendChild(createJobCard(job));
            });
        }

        function getJobText(job, field) {
            const langKey = `${field}_${currentLang}`;
            const fallbackZhKey = `${field}_zh`;
            return job[langKey] || job[fallbackZhKey] || job[field] || '';
        }

        function createJobCard(job) {
            const card = document.createElement('button');
            card.type = 'button';
            card.className = 'job-square light-mode-card-nested rounded-3xl p-5 cursor-pointer h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center group';
            card.style.setProperty('--job-color', job.color);

            const title = getJobText(job, 'title');
            const location = getJobText(job, 'location');
            const hint = currentLang === 'en'
                ? 'Click the card to view job details'
                : '點擊卡片查看職位詳細資訊';

            card.setAttribute('aria-label', currentLang === 'en' ? `View details for ${title}` : `查看 ${title} 職缺詳細資訊`);
            card.innerHTML = `
                <div class="job-icon-badge w-16 h-16 md:w-20 md:h-20 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-5 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                    <i data-lucide="${job.icon}" class="w-9 h-9 md:w-10 md:h-10 text-white"></i>
                </div>
                <h3 class="font-bold text-base md:text-lg leading-tight text-white light-mode-card-title mb-2">${title}</h3>
                <p class="text-xs text-gray-400 light-mode-desc">${location}</p>
                <p class="job-card-hint">${hint}</p>
            `;
            card.addEventListener('click', () => showJobDetailModal(job));
            return card;
        }

        function showJobDetailModal(job) {
            const modal = document.getElementById('jobModal');
            const content = document.getElementById('jobModalContent');
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('jobModalTitle');
            if (!modal || !content || !titleEl) return;

            document.body.classList.add('modal-open');
            const scrollArea = document.querySelector('.content-scroll-area');
            if (scrollArea) {
                scrollArea.classList.add('modal-scroll-locked');
            }

            currentJobDetail = job;
            const title = getJobText(job, 'title');
            const location = getJobText(job, 'location');
            const salary = getJobText(job, 'salary');
            const description = getJobText(job, 'description');
            const detail = getJobText(job, 'detail');

            titleEl.textContent = title;

            const labels = currentLang === 'en'
              ? {
                  voice: 'Voice Guide',
                  close: 'Close',
                  detail: 'Details',
                  location: 'Location',
                  salary: 'Salary',
                  description: 'Responsibilities'
                }
              : {
                  voice: '語音導覽',
                  close: '關閉',
                  detail: '詳細說明',
                  location: '工作地點',
                  salary: '薪資',
                  description: '工作重點'
                };

            const voiceSpan = modal.querySelector('#jobVoiceBtn span');
            if (voiceSpan) voiceSpan.textContent = labels.voice;

            const closeBtn = modal.querySelector('.border-t button');
            if (closeBtn) closeBtn.textContent = labels.close;

            const headerCloseBtn = modal.querySelector('button[aria-label]');
            if (headerCloseBtn) {
                headerCloseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', currentLang === 'en' ? 'Close job detail modal' : '關閉職缺詳細視窗');
            }

            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start gap-6">
                    <div class="job-icon-badge w-20 h-20 rounded-3xl flex-shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center" style="--job-color: ${job.color};">
                        <i data-lucide="${job.icon}" class="w-12 h-12 text-white"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start md:justify-between gap-4">
                            <div>
                                <h3 class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-white light-mode-card-title">${title}</h3>
                                <p class="font-medium mt-1" style="color: ${job.color};">${location}</p>
                            </div>
                            <span class="inline-block px-4 py-1.5 bg-emerald-500/12 border border-emerald-400/30 text-emerald-300 text-sm font-semibold rounded-2xl">${salary}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="my-7 border-l-4 pl-5" style="border-color: ${job.color};">
                            <p class="text-gray-300 light-mode-desc leading-relaxed">${description}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div class="bg-gray-900/70 light-mode-card-nested rounded-2xl p-5 text-sm border border-gray-800 light-mode-border">
                            <p class="font-semibold text-white light-mode-card-title mb-2">${labels.detail}</p>
                            <p class="text-gray-300 light-mode-desc leading-relaxed">${detail}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;

            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');
            if (window.lucide) {
                lucide.createIcons();
            }
        }

        function closeJobModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('jobModal');
            if (!modal) return;

            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');
            const scrollArea = document.querySelector('.content-scroll-area');
            if (scrollArea) {
                scrollArea.classList.remove('modal-scroll-locked');
            }

            currentJobDetail = null;
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
        }

        function startJobAICoach() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('jobModal');
            const content = document.getElementById('jobModalContent');
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('jobModalTitle');
            if (!modal || !content || !titleEl) return;

            document.body.classList.add('modal-open');
            const scrollArea = document.querySelector('.content-scroll-area');
            if (scrollArea) {
                scrollArea.classList.add('modal-scroll-locked');
            }

            currentJobDetail = null;

            const isEn = currentLang === 'en';
            titleEl.textContent = isEn ? 'AI Interview Coach' : 'AI 面試教練';

            const labels = isEn
              ? {
                  voice: 'Voice Guide',
                  close: 'Close'
                }
              : {
                  voice: '語音導覽',
                  close: '關閉'
                };

            const voiceSpan = modal.querySelector('#jobVoiceBtn span');
            if (voiceSpan) voiceSpan.textContent = labels.voice;

            const closeBtn = modal.querySelector('.border-t button');
            if (closeBtn) closeBtn.textContent = labels.close;

            const headerCloseBtn = modal.querySelector('button[aria-label]');
            if (headerCloseBtn) {
                headerCloseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', isEn ? 'Close job detail modal' : '關閉職缺詳細視窗');
            }

            content.innerHTML = isEn ? `
                <div class="text-center py-8">
                    <i data-lucide="bot" class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto text-cyan-400 mb-6"></i>
                    <h3 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white light-mode-card-title mb-4">Start UMC Interview Practice</h3>
                    <p class="text-gray-400 light-mode-desc mb-8 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                        Select a target position and use the job description as interview practice material to prepare effectively.
                    </p>
                    <button onclick="alert('The AI interview practice feature will connect to custom question banks or generative AI. This is a demo version.')" class="bg-blue-600 text-white px-10 py-4 rounded-2xl font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
                        Start Practice
                    </button>
                </div>
            ` : `
                <div class="text-center py-8">
                    <i data-lucide="bot" class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto text-cyan-400 mb-6"></i>
                    <h3 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white light-mode-card-title mb-4">準備開始 UMC 面試練習</h3>
                    <p class="text-gray-400 light-mode-desc mb-8 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                        選擇目標職缺後，您可以用職缺內容作為面試練習素材，聚焦工作重點、薪資與職能條件進行準備。
                    </p>
                    <button onclick="alert('AI 面試練習功能可接續串接題庫或生成式 AI，目前為展示版。')" class="bg-blue-600 text-white px-10 py-4 rounded-2xl font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 transition">
                        開始練習
                    </button>
                </div>
            `;
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');
            if (window.lucide) {
                lucide.createIcons();
            }
        }

        function jobSalaryToSpoken(salary) {
            return salary
                .replace(/\$/g, '新台幣')
                .replace(/55,000/g, '五萬五千')
                .replace(/75,000/g, '七萬五千')
                .replace(/38,000/g, '三萬八千')
                .replace(/45,000/g, '四萬五千')
                .replace(/50,000/g, '五萬元')
                .replace(/70,000/g, '七萬元')
                .replace(/52,000/g, '五萬二千')
                .replace(/57,000/g, '五萬七千')
                .replace(/60,000/g, '六萬元')
                .replace(/39,300/g, '三萬九千三百')
                .replace(/40,000/g, '四萬元')
                .replace(/85萬/g, '八十五萬')
                .replace(/100萬/g, '一百萬');
        }

        let availableVoices = [];

        function loadSpeechVoices() {
            if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return [];
            availableVoices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
            return availableVoices;
        }

        function getPreferredVoice(lang) {
            if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return null;

            const voices = availableVoices.length ? availableVoices : loadSpeechVoices();

            if (lang === 'en') {
                return (
                    voices.find(voice => voice.name.includes('British-Male')) ||
                    voices.find(voice => {
                        const name = voice.name.toLowerCase();
                        return name.includes('british') && name.includes('male');
                    }) ||
                    voices.find(voice => voice.lang === 'en-GB') ||
                    voices.find(voice => voice.lang.startsWith('en')) ||
                    null
                );
            }

            return (
                voices.find(voice => voice.name.includes('Taiwan-Yating')) ||
                voices.find(voice => voice.name.includes('Yating')) ||
                voices.find(voice => voice.lang === 'zh-TW') ||
                voices.find(voice => voice.lang.startsWith('zh')) ||
                null
            );
        }

        function applyPreferredSpeechVoice(utterance, lang, options = {}) {
            const isEn = lang === 'en';
            utterance.lang = isEn ? 'en-GB' : 'zh-TW';

            const preferredVoice = getPreferredVoice(isEn ? 'en' : 'zh');
            if (preferredVoice) {
                utterance.voice = preferredVoice;
            }

            utterance.rate = options.rate ?? (isEn ? 0.95 : 1.05);
            utterance.pitch = options.pitch ?? 1.0;
            utterance.volume = options.volume ?? 0.95;
        }

        function speakTextWithPreferredVoice(text, lang, options = {}) {
            if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
                alert(currentLang === 'en'
                    ? 'Your browser does not support voice guide.'
                    : '您的瀏覽器不支援語音導覽功能');
                return;
            }

            stopAllVoiceGuide();

            const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
            applyPreferredSpeechVoice(utterance, lang, options);
            window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
        }

        if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
            loadSpeechVoices();
            window.speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = loadSpeechVoices;
            window.setTimeout(loadSpeechVoices, 100);
            window.setTimeout(loadSpeechVoices, 500);
        }

        function speakJobDetail(job) {
            if (!job || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;

            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            const title = getJobText(job, 'title');
            const location = getJobText(job, 'location');
            const salary = getJobText(job, 'salary');
            const description = getJobText(job, 'description');
            const detail = getJobText(job, 'detail');

            let text, lang;
            if (currentLang === 'en') {
                text = `${title}. Job location: ${location}. Salary range: ${salary}. Key responsibilities: ${description}. Job details: ${detail}.`;
                lang = 'en';
            } else {
                const spokenSalary = jobSalaryToSpoken(salary);
                text = `${title}。工作地點：${location}。薪資範圍：${spokenSalary}。工作重點：${description}。詳細說明：${detail}。`;
                lang = 'zh';
            }
            const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
            applyPreferredSpeechVoice(utterance, lang);
            window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
            if (!(event.target instanceof Element)) return;

            const voiceButton = event.target.closest('#jobVoiceBtn');
            if (voiceButton) {
                speakJobDetail(currentJobDetail);
                return;
            }

            const modal = document.getElementById('jobModal');
            if (modal && !modal.classList.contains('hidden') && event.target === modal) {
                closeJobModal();
            }

            const intModal = document.getElementById('interviewPracticeModal');
            if (intModal && !intModal.classList.contains('hidden') && event.target === intModal) {
                closeInterviewPracticeModal();
            }
        });

        // AI 面試題庫資料與函式

        function getInterviewLang() {
            return currentLang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'zh';
        }

        function getInterviewText(item, field) {
            const lang = getInterviewLang();
            return item[`${field}_${lang}`] || item[`${field}_zh`] || '';
        }

        function getInterviewLabels() {
            return getInterviewLang() === 'en'
                ? {
                    modalTitle: 'AI Interview Practice Bank',
                    modalSubtitle: 'Choose a question category to start practicing',
                    questionListSubtitle: 'Select a question to start practicing.',
                    practiceHint: 'Answer this question verbally or in your mind first, then click "View Suggested Answer".',
                    viewTip: 'View Suggested Answer',
                    back: 'Back',
                    close: 'Close',
                    questions: 'questions',
                    answerTip: 'Use the STAR structure: explain the Situation, Task, Action, and Result. For technical questions, start with the core concept, then connect it to your project, internship, or coursework experience and explain how it relates to the semiconductor industry.'
                  }
                : {
                    modalTitle: 'AI 面試題庫選擇',
                    modalSubtitle: '請選擇想練習的面試問題類型',
                    questionListSubtitle: '以下是此分類常見面試題，請選擇一題開始練習。',
                    practiceHint: '請先在心中或口頭回答這題，再點擊「查看回答建議」。',
                    viewTip: '查看回答建議',
                    back: '返回',
                    close: '關閉',
                    questions: '題',
                    answerTip: '回答時建議使用 STAR 架構：先說明情境（Situation），再描述任務（Task）、你的行動（Action），最後補充結果（Result）。若是專業題，請先講核心概念，再用自身專題、實習或課程經驗連結到半導體產業。'
                  };
        }

        function openInterviewPracticeModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('interviewPracticeModal');
            if (!modal) return;

            renderInterviewCategorySelection();

            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');
            document.body.classList.add('modal-open');

            const scrollArea = document.querySelector('.content-scroll-area');
            if (scrollArea) scrollArea.classList.add('modal-scroll-locked');
        }

        function closeInterviewPracticeModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('interviewPracticeModal');
            if (!modal) return;

            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');
            document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');

            const scrollArea = document.querySelector('.content-scroll-area');
            if (scrollArea) scrollArea.classList.remove('modal-scroll-locked');
        }

        function setInterviewModalHeader(title, subtitle) {
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('interviewModalTitle');
            const subtitleEl = document.getElementById('interviewModalSubtitle');

            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = title;
            if (subtitleEl) subtitleEl.textContent = subtitle;
        }

        function renderInterviewCategorySelection() {
            const content = document.getElementById('interviewModalContent');
            if (!content) return;

            const labels = getInterviewLabels();
            setInterviewModalHeader(labels.modalTitle, labels.modalSubtitle);

            const entries = Object.entries(interviewQuestionBank);

            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    ${entries.map(([key, item]) => {
                        const title = getInterviewText(item, 'title');
                        const description = getInterviewText(item, 'description');
                        const questions = item.questions || [];

                        return `
                            <button type="button" class="interview-category-card" onclick="renderInterviewQuestionList('${key}')">
                                <h4>${title}</h4>
                                <p>${description}</p>
                                <span class="interview-count-badge">${questions.length} ${labels.questions}</span>
                            </button>
                        `;
                    }).join('')}
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function renderInterviewQuestionList(categoryKey) {
            const content = document.getElementById('interviewModalContent');
            const category = interviewQuestionBank[categoryKey];
            if (!content || !category) return;

            const labels = getInterviewLabels();
            const title = getInterviewText(category, 'title');
            const questions = category.questions || [];

            setInterviewModalHeader(title, labels.questionListSubtitle);

            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="space-y-3">
                    ${questions.map((item, index) => {
                        const questionText = item[`question_${getInterviewLang()}`] || item.question_zh || '';
                        return `
                            <button type="button" class="interview-question-card" onclick="renderInterviewQuestionPractice('${categoryKey}', ${index})">
                                <h4>${index + 1}. ${questionText}</h4>
                            </button>
                        `;
                    }).join('')}
                </div>

                <div class="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3 justify-end">
                    <button type="button" class="interview-secondary-btn" onclick="renderInterviewCategorySelection()">${labels.back}</button>
                    <button type="button" class="interview-secondary-btn" onclick="closeInterviewPracticeModal()">${labels.close}</button>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function renderInterviewQuestionPractice(categoryKey, questionIndex) {
            const content = document.getElementById('interviewModalContent');
            const category = interviewQuestionBank[categoryKey];
            if (!content || !category) return;

            const labels = getInterviewLabels();
            const title = getInterviewText(category, 'title');
            const questionItem = category.questions?.[questionIndex];

            if (!questionItem) return;

            const questionText = questionItem[`question_${getInterviewLang()}`] || questionItem.question_zh || '';

            setInterviewModalHeader(title, labels.practiceHint);

            content.innerHTML = `
                <div class="interview-question-card">
                    <h4>${questionIndex + 1}. ${questionText}</h4>
                    <p>${labels.practiceHint}</p>
                </div>

                <div id="interviewAnswerTipBox"></div>

                <div class="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3 justify-end">
                    <button type="button" class="interview-action-btn" onclick="showInterviewAnswerTip('${categoryKey}', ${questionIndex})">${labels.viewTip}</button>
                    <button type="button" class="interview-secondary-btn" onclick="renderInterviewQuestionList('${categoryKey}')">${labels.back}</button>
                    <button type="button" class="interview-secondary-btn" onclick="closeInterviewPracticeModal()">${labels.close}</button>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function showInterviewAnswerTip(categoryKey, questionIndex) {
            const box = document.getElementById('interviewAnswerTipBox');
            const category = interviewQuestionBank[categoryKey];
            if (!box || !category) return;

            const questionItem = category.questions?.[questionIndex];
            if (!questionItem) return;

            const lang = getInterviewLang();
            const customTip = questionItem[`tip_${lang}`] || questionItem.tip_zh || getInterviewLabels().answerTip;

            box.innerHTML = `
                <div class="interview-answer-tip">
                    ${customTip}
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function initPestelFlipCards() {
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.pestel-flip-card');

            cards.forEach(card => {
                if (card.dataset.pestelFlipBound === 'true') return;
                card.dataset.pestelFlipBound = 'true';

                function toggleCard(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();

                    card.classList.toggle('flipped');
                    card.setAttribute(
                        'aria-pressed',
                        card.classList.contains('flipped') ? 'true' : 'false'
                    );
                }

                card.addEventListener('pointerup', toggleCard);

                card.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                        toggleCard(event);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        function initDynamicFlipCards() {
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.dynamic-flip-card');

            cards.forEach(card => {
                if (card.dataset.dynamicFlipBound === 'true') return;
                card.dataset.dynamicFlipBound = 'true';

                function toggleCard(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();

                    card.classList.toggle('flipped');
                    card.setAttribute(
                        'aria-pressed',
                        card.classList.contains('flipped') ? 'true' : 'false'
                    );
                }

                card.addEventListener('pointerup', toggleCard);

                card.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                        toggleCard(event);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        function initDynamicCompetitorReveal() {
            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.dynamic-competitor-row');

            rows.forEach(row => {
                if (row.dataset.dynamicRevealBound === 'true') return;
                row.dataset.dynamicRevealBound = 'true';

                function toggleRow(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();

                    const isActive = row.classList.toggle('is-active');
                    row.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');
                }

                row.addEventListener('click', toggleRow);

                row.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                        toggleRow(event);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        function initComplementaryFlipCards() {
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.complementary-flip-card');

            cards.forEach(card => {

                card.dataset.complementaryFlipBound = 'true';

                        'aria-pressed',
                        card.classList.contains('flipped') ? 'true' : 'false'
                    );

                card.addEventListener('pointerup', toggleCard);

                card.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                        toggleCard(event);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

                function toggleCard(event) {

                    card.classList.toggle('flipped');
                    card.setAttribute(
                        'aria-pressed',
                        card.classList.contains('flipped') ? 'true' : 'false'
                    );
                }

                card.addEventListener('pointerup', toggleCard);

                card.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                        toggleCard(event);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        function initBmcFlipCards() {
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.bmc-flip-card');

            cards.forEach(card => {
                if (card.dataset.bmcFlipBound === 'true') return;
                card.dataset.bmcFlipBound = 'true';

                function toggleCard(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();

                    card.classList.toggle('flipped');
                    card.setAttribute(
                        'aria-pressed',
                        card.classList.contains('flipped') ? 'true' : 'false'
                    );
                }

                card.addEventListener('pointerup', toggleCard);

                card.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                        toggleCard(event);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        function initStrategyGroupHighlight() {
            const analyses = document.querySelectorAll('.strategy-analysis');
            if (!analyses.length) return;

            analyses.forEach(analysis => {
                if (analysis.dataset.highlightBound === 'true') return;
                analysis.dataset.highlightBound = 'true';

                const zones = analysis.querySelectorAll('[data-group-zone]');
                const cards = analysis.querySelectorAll('[data-group-card]');
                const clusters = analysis.querySelectorAll('[data-group-cluster]');
                const lines = analysis.querySelectorAll('[data-group-line]');
                const triggers = analysis.querySelectorAll('[data-group-zone], [data-group-cluster], [data-group-card], [data-strategy-group]');

                function getGroup(element) {
                    return element.dataset.groupZone
                        || element.dataset.groupCluster
                        || element.dataset.groupCard
                        || element.dataset.strategyGroup;
                }

                function resetGroups() {
                    analysis.classList.remove('has-active-group', 'strategy-has-active');

                    zones.forEach(zone => {
                        zone.classList.remove('active', 'is-active', 'is-dimmed');
                    });

                    cards.forEach(card => {
                        card.classList.remove('active', 'is-active', 'is-dimmed');
                    });

                    clusters.forEach(cluster => {
                        cluster.classList.remove('active', 'is-active', 'is-dimmed');
                    });

                    lines.forEach(line => {
                        line.classList.remove('active', 'is-active', 'is-dimmed');
                    });
                }

                function activateGroup(group) {
                    if (!group) {
                        resetGroups();
                        return;
                    }

                    analysis.classList.add('has-active-group', 'strategy-has-active');

                    zones.forEach(zone => {
                        const isActive = zone.dataset.groupZone === group;
                        zone.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                        zone.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
                        zone.classList.toggle('is-dimmed', !isActive);
                    });

                    cards.forEach(card => {
                        const isActive = card.dataset.groupCard === group;
                        card.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                        card.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
                        card.classList.toggle('is-dimmed', !isActive);
                    });

                    clusters.forEach(cluster => {
                        const isActive = cluster.dataset.groupCluster === group;
                        cluster.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                        cluster.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
                        cluster.classList.toggle('is-dimmed', !isActive);
                    });

                    lines.forEach(line => {
                        const isActive = line.dataset.groupLine === group;
                        line.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                        line.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
                        line.classList.toggle('is-dimmed', !isActive);
                    });
                }

                triggers.forEach(trigger => {
                    const group = getGroup(trigger);

                    trigger.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => activateGroup(group));
                    trigger.addEventListener('focus', () => activateGroup(group));
                    trigger.addEventListener('click', event => {
                        event.preventDefault();
                        event.stopPropagation();
                        activateGroup(group);
                    });
                    trigger.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                        if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return;
                        event.preventDefault();
                        activateGroup(group);
                    });
                });

                analysis.addEventListener('mouseleave', resetGroups);

                document.addEventListener('click', event => {
                    if (!analysis.contains(event.target)) {
                        resetGroups();
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        function scrollContentToTop() {
            const scrollArea = document.getElementById('content-scroll-area');
            if (scrollArea) {
                scrollArea.scrollTop = 0;
            }
        }

        function updateSectionStatus(item) {
            const sectionTitle = document.getElementById('current-section-title');
            if (sectionTitle) {
                sectionTitle.innerText = currentLang === 'zh' ? item.title_zh : item.title_en;
            }

            const sectionIndex = document.getElementById('current-section-index');
            if (sectionIndex) {
                sectionIndex.innerText = `${activeIndex + 1} / ${SECTIONS.length}`;
            }
        }

        function renderSectionQuizContainer(section) {
            if (!section || !section.quizId || !sectionQuizzes[section.quizId]) return '';
            return `<div class="section-quiz" data-quiz-id="${section.quizId}"></div>`;
        }

        function getSectionDisplayContent(section) {
            if (!section) return '';
            const langKey = currentLang === 'en' ? 'content_en' : 'content_zh';
            const baseContent = section[langKey] || section.content_zh || '';
            return `${baseContent}${renderSectionQuizContainer(section)}`;
        }

        function initSectionQuizzes() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.section-quiz').forEach(container => {
                const quizId = container.dataset.quizId;
                const quiz = sectionQuizzes[quizId];
                if (!quiz) return;

                renderSectionQuiz(container, quizId, quiz);
            });
        }

        function renderSectionQuiz(container, quizId, quiz) {
            const lang = currentLang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'zh';
            const title = quiz[`title_${lang}`] || quiz.title_zh;
            const questions = quiz.questions || [];
            const intro = lang === 'en'
                ? 'Answer the questions based on this section.'
                : '請根據本章節內容回答以下問題。';

            container.innerHTML = `
                <div class="quiz-card">
                    <div class="quiz-header">
                        <div>
                            <div class="quiz-title-row">
                                <h3>${title}</h3>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    class="section-voice-btn"
                                    onclick="event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); speakCurrentQuiz('${quizId}')"
                                    aria-label="${lang === 'en' ? 'Voice Guide' : '語音導覽'}"
                                >
                                    <i data-lucide="volume-2"></i>
                                    <span data-zh="語音導覽" data-en="Voice Guide">${lang === 'en' ? 'Voice Guide' : '語音導覽'}</span>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <p>${intro}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div class="quiz-score" id="quiz-score-${quizId}">
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Not submitted' : '尚未作答'}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="quiz-questions">
                        ${questions.map((q, qIndex) => {
                            const questionText = q[`question_${lang}`] || q.question_zh;
                            const options = q[`options_${lang}`] || q.options_zh || [];

                            return `
                                <div class="quiz-question" data-question-index="${qIndex}">
                                    <div class="quiz-question-heading">
                                        <h4>${qIndex + 1}. ${questionText}</h4>
                                        <button
                                            type="button"
                                            class="quiz-question-voice-btn"
                                            onclick="event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); speakQuizQuestion('${quizId}', ${qIndex})"
                                            aria-label="${lang === 'en' ? 'Voice Guide' : '語音導覽'}"
                                        >
                                            <i data-lucide="volume-2"></i>
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="quiz-options">
                                        ${options.map((option, optionIndex) => `
                                            <label class="quiz-option">
                                                <input type="radio" name="quiz-${quizId}-${qIndex}" value="${optionIndex}">
                                                <span>${option}</span>
                                            </label>
                                        `).join('')}
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="quiz-feedback" id="quiz-feedback-${quizId}-${qIndex}"></div>
                                </div>
                            `;
                        }).join('')}
                    </div>

                    <div class="quiz-actions">
                        <button type="button" class="quiz-submit-btn" onclick="submitSectionQuiz('${quizId}')">
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Submit Answers' : '提交答案'}
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" class="quiz-reset-btn" onclick="resetSectionQuiz('${quizId}')">
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Reset' : '重新作答'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function getSelectedQuizAnswer(quizId, questionIndex) {
            const selected = document.querySelector(`input[name="quiz-${quizId}-${questionIndex}"]:checked`);
            return selected ? Number(selected.value) : null;
        }

        function buildQuizQuestionVoiceText(quizId, questionIndex) {
            const quiz = sectionQuizzes[quizId];
            if (!quiz || !quiz.questions || !quiz.questions[questionIndex]) return '';

            const isEn = currentLang === 'en';
            const lang = isEn ? 'en' : 'zh';
            const q = quiz.questions[questionIndex];
            const title = quiz[`title_${lang}`] || quiz.title_zh || (isEn ? 'Quiz' : '章節測驗');
            const questionText = q[`question_${lang}`] || q.question_zh;
            const options = q[`options_${lang}`] || q.options_zh || [];
            const explanation = q[`explanation_${lang}`] || q.explanation_zh || '';
            const correctIndex = q.answerIndex;
            const selectedIndex = getSelectedQuizAnswer(quizId, questionIndex);
            const optionLabels = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E', 'F'];

            let text = '';

            if (isEn) {
                text += `${title}. Question ${questionIndex + 1}. ${questionText}. `;
                options.forEach((option, index) => {
                    text += `Option ${optionLabels[index]}, ${option}. `;
                });

                if (selectedIndex === null) {
                    text += 'Please choose the answer you think is correct.';
                    return text;
                }

                text += `Your answer is option ${optionLabels[selectedIndex]}, ${options[selectedIndex]}. `;
                text += selectedIndex === correctIndex ? 'That is correct. ' : 'That is incorrect. ';
                text += `The correct answer is option ${optionLabels[correctIndex]}, ${options[correctIndex]}. `;
                text += `Explanation: ${explanation}`;
                return text;
            }

            text += `${title}。第 ${questionIndex + 1} 題。${questionText}。`;
            options.forEach((option, index) => {
                text += `選項 ${optionLabels[index]}，${option}。`;
            });

            if (selectedIndex === null) {
                text += '請選擇你認為正確的答案。';
                return text;
            }

            text += `你選擇的是選項 ${optionLabels[selectedIndex]}，${options[selectedIndex]}。`;
            text += selectedIndex === correctIndex ? '答對了。' : '答錯了。';
            text += `正確答案是選項 ${optionLabels[correctIndex]}，${options[correctIndex]}。`;
            text += `原因是，${explanation}`;
            return text;
        }

        function speakQuizQuestion(quizId, questionIndex) {
            const text = buildQuizQuestionVoiceText(quizId, questionIndex);
            if (!text) return;
            speakTextWithPreferredVoice(text, currentLang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'zh');
        }

        function speakCurrentQuiz(quizId) {
            const quiz = sectionQuizzes[quizId];
            if (!quiz) return;

            const text = (quiz.questions || [])
                .map((q, questionIndex) => buildQuizQuestionVoiceText(quizId, questionIndex))
                .filter(Boolean)
                .join(currentLang === 'en' ? ' ' : ' ');

            if (!text) return;
            speakTextWithPreferredVoice(text, currentLang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'zh');
        }

        function submitSectionQuiz(quizId) {
            const quiz = sectionQuizzes[quizId];
            if (!quiz) return;

            const lang = currentLang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'zh';
            const questions = quiz.questions || [];
            let correctCount = 0;
            let answeredCount = 0;

            questions.forEach((q, qIndex) => {
                const selected = document.querySelector(`input[name="quiz-${quizId}-${qIndex}"]:checked`);
                const feedback = document.getElementById(`quiz-feedback-${quizId}-${qIndex}`);
                const options = q[`options_${lang}`] || q.options_zh || [];
                const explanation = q[`explanation_${lang}`] || q.explanation_zh;
                const correctAnswer = options[q.answerIndex];

                if (!feedback) return;

                if (!selected) {
                    feedback.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="quiz-feedback-box quiz-feedback-warning">
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Please select an answer.' : '請先選擇一個答案。'}
                        </div>
                    `;
                    return;
                }

                answeredCount += 1;

                const selectedIndex = Number(selected.value);

                if (selectedIndex === q.answerIndex) {
                    correctCount += 1;
                    feedback.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="quiz-feedback-box quiz-feedback-correct">
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Correct!' : '答對了！'}
                        </div>
                    `;
                } else {
                    feedback.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="quiz-feedback-box quiz-feedback-wrong">
                            <strong>${lang === 'en' ? 'Incorrect.' : '答錯了。'}</strong>
                            <br>
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Correct answer: ' : '正確答案：'}${correctAnswer}
                            <br>
                            ${lang === 'en' ? 'Why: ' : '原因：'}${explanation}
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
            });

            const scoreEl = document.getElementById(`quiz-score-${quizId}`);
            if (!scoreEl) return;

            if (answeredCount < questions.length) {
                scoreEl.textContent = currentLang === 'en'
                    ? `Answered: ${answeredCount} / ${questions.length}`
                    : `已作答：${answeredCount} / ${questions.length}`;
                return;
            }

            const score = questions.length ? Math.round((correctCount / questions.length) * 100) : 0;
            scoreEl.textContent = currentLang === 'en'
                ? `Score: ${score} / 100`
                : `分數：${score} / 100`;
        }

        function resetSectionQuiz(quizId) {
            const quiz = sectionQuizzes[quizId];
            if (!quiz) return;

            (quiz.questions || []).forEach((q, qIndex) => {
                document.querySelectorAll(`input[name="quiz-${quizId}-${qIndex}"]`).forEach(input => {
                    input.checked = false;
                });

                const feedback = document.getElementById(`quiz-feedback-${quizId}-${qIndex}`);
                if (feedback) feedback.innerHTML = '';
            });

            const scoreEl = document.getElementById(`quiz-score-${quizId}`);
            if (scoreEl) {
                scoreEl.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? 'Not submitted' : '尚未作答';
            }
        }
