            { key: 'jobs-tech', selector: '#job-category-4' },
            { key: 'jobs-fab', selector: '#job-category-5' }
        ];

        const bmcCardVoiceTargets = {
            '關鍵合作夥伴': 'bmc-key-partners',
            '關鍵活動': 'bmc-key-activities',
            '核心資源': 'bmc-key-resources',
            '價值主張': 'bmc-value-propositions',
            '客戶關係': 'bmc-customer-relationships',
            '通路管道': 'bmc-channels',
            '客戶細分': 'bmc-customer-segments',
            '成本結構': 'bmc-cost-structure',
            '收益流': 'bmc-revenue-streams'
        };

        function stopAllVoiceGuide() {
            if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
                window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
            }
        }

        function speakSectionVoice(sectionKey) {
            if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
                alert(currentLang === 'en'
                    ? 'Your browser does not support voice guide.'
                    : '您的瀏覽器不支援語音導覽功能');
                return;
            }

            const guide = sectionVoiceGuides[sectionKey];
            if (!guide) return;

            const isEn = currentLang === 'en';
            const text = isEn ? guide.en : guide.zh;

            stopAllVoiceGuide();

            const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
            applyPreferredSpeechVoice(utterance, isEn ? 'en' : 'zh');

            window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
        }

        function createSectionVoiceButton(sectionKey, compact = false) {
            const button = document.createElement('button');
            button.type = 'button';
            button.className = `section-voice-btn${compact ? ' section-voice-btn-compact' : ''}`;
            button.setAttribute('aria-label', currentLang === 'en' ? 'Voice Guide' : '語音導覽');
            button.dataset.sectionVoiceKey = sectionKey;

            const icon = document.createElement('i');
            icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'volume-2');

            const label = document.createElement('span');
            label.dataset.zh = '語音導覽';
            label.dataset.en = 'Voice Guide';
            label.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? 'Voice Guide' : '語音導覽';

            button.append(icon, label);

            button.addEventListener('click', event => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
                speakSectionVoice(sectionKey);
            });

            button.addEventListener('pointerup', event => {
                event.stopPropagation();
            });

            button.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();
                    speakSectionVoice(sectionKey);
                }
            });

            return button;
        }

        function wrapHeadingWithVoice(heading, sectionKey, compact = false) {
            if (!heading || heading.dataset.voiceEnhanced === 'true') return;

            const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
            wrapper.className = `section-heading-with-voice${compact ? ' section-heading-with-voice-compact' : ''}`;

            const marginClass = Array.from(heading.classList).find(className => /^mb-\d+$/.test(className));
            if (marginClass) {
                heading.classList.remove(marginClass);
                wrapper.classList.add(marginClass);
            }

            heading.parentNode.insertBefore(wrapper, heading);
            wrapper.appendChild(heading);
            wrapper.appendChild(createSectionVoiceButton(sectionKey, compact));

            heading.dataset.voiceEnhanced = 'true';
        }

        function initSectionVoiceButtons() {
            const root = document.getElementById('content-display-box');
            if (!root) return;

            sectionVoiceTargets.forEach(target => {
                if (target.selector.startsWith('#job-category-')) {
                    const categoryGrid = root.querySelector(target.selector);
                    const heading = categoryGrid?.previousElementSibling?.matches('h3')
                        ? categoryGrid.previousElementSibling
                        : null;
                    wrapHeadingWithVoice(heading, target.key);
                    return;
                }

                root.querySelectorAll(target.selector).forEach(heading => {
                    const compact = Boolean(heading.closest('.bmc-card-front'));
                    wrapHeadingWithVoice(heading, target.key, compact);
                });
            });
        }

        function createBmcCardVoiceButton(sectionKey) {
            const button = document.createElement('button');
            button.type = 'button';
            button.className = 'bmc-card-voice-btn';
            button.setAttribute('aria-label', currentLang === 'en' ? 'Voice Guide' : '語音導覽');
            button.dataset.sectionVoiceKey = sectionKey;

            const icon = document.createElement('i');
            icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'volume-2');
            button.appendChild(icon);

            button.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
                event.stopPropagation();
            });

            button.addEventListener('pointerup', event => {
                event.stopPropagation();
            });

            button.addEventListener('click', event => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();
                speakSectionVoice(sectionKey);
            });

            button.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();
                    speakSectionVoice(sectionKey);
                }
            });

            return button;
        }

        function initBmcCardVoiceButtons() {
            const root = document.getElementById('content-display-box');
            if (!root) return;

            root.querySelectorAll('#market-bmc .bmc-card-front').forEach(cardFront => {
                if (cardFront.dataset.voiceEnhanced === 'true') return;

                const title = cardFront.querySelector('h4[data-zh]');
                const sectionKey = title ? bmcCardVoiceTargets[title.dataset.zh] : null;
                if (!sectionKey) return;

                cardFront.appendChild(createBmcCardVoiceButton(sectionKey));
                cardFront.dataset.voiceEnhanced = 'true';
            });
        }

        function initSectionInteractiveBlocks() {
            initSectionVoiceButtons();
            initBmcCardVoiceButtons();
            initPestelFlipCards();
            initDynamicFlipCards();
            initDynamicCompetitorReveal();
            initComplementaryFlipCards();
            initSixForceFlipCards();
            initBmcFlipCards();
            initStrategyGroupHighlight();
            initJobsSection();
            initSectionQuizzes();
            if (window.lucide) {
                lucide.createIcons();
            }
        }

        function enterCurrentSection() {
            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            const item = SECTIONS[activeIndex];
            updateSectionStatus(item);
            document.getElementById('content-display-box').innerHTML = getSectionDisplayContent(item);
            setTimeout(() => {
                initSectionInteractiveBlocks();
            }, 0);

            const landing = document.getElementById('landing-page');
            const content = document.getElementById('content-container');
            landing.style.transform = 'scale(0.9) translateZ(-100px)';
            landing.style.opacity = '0';
            
            setTimeout(() => {
                landing.classList.add('hidden');
                content.classList.remove('hidden');
                scrollContentToTop();
                updateBrandTitle();
            }, 300);
        }

        function switchContentSection(direction) {
            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            let nextIdx = activeIndex + direction;
            if (nextIdx < 0) nextIdx = SECTIONS.length - 1;
            if (nextIdx >= SECTIONS.length) nextIdx = 0;
            activeIndex = nextIdx;
            
            updateCarousel();
            const item = SECTIONS[activeIndex];
            updateSectionStatus(item);
            
            const displayBox = document.getElementById('content-display-box');
            displayBox.style.opacity = '0';
            setTimeout(() => {
                displayBox.innerHTML = getSectionDisplayContent(item);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    initSectionInteractiveBlocks();
                }, 0);
                displayBox.style.opacity = '1';
                scrollContentToTop();
            }, 150);
        }

        function goToHome() {
            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            const landing = document.getElementById('landing-page');
            const content = document.getElementById('content-container');
            content.classList.add('hidden');
            landing.classList.remove('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => {
                landing.style.transform = 'scale(1) translateZ(0)';
                landing.style.opacity = '1';
                updateCarousel();
            }, 50);
        }

        // 明暗模式切換邏輯
        function toggleDarkMode() {
            const body = document.getElementById('app-body');
            
            body.classList.toggle('light-mode');
            
            const isLight = body.classList.contains('light-mode');
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-icon="moon"]').forEach(icon => {
                icon.classList.toggle('hidden', isLight);
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-icon="sun"]').forEach(icon => {
                icon.classList.toggle('hidden', !isLight);
            });
        }

        // 中英切換邏輯
        function toggleLanguage() {
            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            currentLang = currentLang === 'zh' ? 'en' : 'zh';
            document.body.classList.toggle('lang-en', currentLang === 'en');
            
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-lang-toggle]').forEach(btn => {
                btn.innerText = currentLang === 'zh' ? '中/EN' : 'EN/中';
            });
            
            const elements = document.querySelectorAll('[data-zh]');
            elements.forEach(el => {
                el.innerText = currentLang === 'zh' ? el.getAttribute('data-zh') : el.getAttribute('data-en');
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('[data-zh][data-en]').forEach(el => {
                el.textContent = currentLang === 'en' ? el.dataset.en : el.dataset.zh;
            });
            
            initCarousel();
            
            const isContentOpen = !document.getElementById('content-container').classList.contains('hidden');
            if (isContentOpen) {
                const item = SECTIONS[activeIndex];
                updateSectionStatus(item);
                document.getElementById('content-display-box').innerHTML = getSectionDisplayContent(item);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    initSectionInteractiveBlocks();
                }, 0);
            }
            if (typeof initJobsSection === 'function') {
                initJobsSection();
            }
        }

        // 語音朗讀功能實裝 (Web Speech API)
        function speakCurrentCard() {
            if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
                alert("抱歉，您的瀏覽器不支援語音合成功能！");
                return;
            }
            stopAllVoiceGuide();
            
            const currentItem = SECTIONS[activeIndex];
            let textToSpeak = "";
            let speechLang = "zh";
            
            if (currentLang === 'zh') {
                textToSpeak = `當前章節：${currentItem.title_zh}。`;
                speechLang = "zh";
            } else {
                textToSpeak = `Current section is: ${currentItem.title_en}.`;
                speechLang = "en";
            }
            
            const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(textToSpeak);
            applyPreferredSpeechVoice(utterance, speechLang);
            
            window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
        }

        window.onload = function() {
            initCarousel();
            window.focus();
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
